Steam games by 4:3 / arbitrary aspect-ratio support
Source list: steam-games.txt (1770 games)
Generated: 2026-06-05T17:21:00.884Z

Purpose: identify which owned Steam games are plausible for a 3.5 inch 640×480 streamed handheld screen without considering game hardware requirements.

Sources:
- Steam Curator “4|3 fullscreen aspect ratio” for explicit 4:3 supported / not-supported / caveat verdicts.
- PCGamingWiki Video table for inferred flexible aspect handling via ultrawide or multi-monitor support.
- steam-games-3.5-inch-640x480.txt fit score, when present, only controls ordering inside each group.

Important caveat: PCGamingWiki ultrawide/multi-monitor support is not the same as confirmed 4:3 support. It is listed separately as “likely arbitrary/flexible” so it can be tried, not trusted blindly.

Counts:
- Confirmed 4:3 supported: 246
- Partial / quirky 4:3 behavior: 8
- Likely arbitrary/flexible aspect support: 477
- Widescreen support only / 4:3 unknown: 457
- Fixed/limited aspect signal: 54
- Confirmed not 4:3 supported: 98
- Unknown: 430
